# Operad receipts, sources and snapshots audit

Completed622/622 assigned paths:351 full-source receipts and271 bounded artifact assessments; zero pending. Scope:9,392,180 bytes. Full-source coverage includes explicit complete-base/every-difference and exact-equality transfers with capture context. No historical program, native binary or provider run was repeated.

The reusable contribution is typed encoding and evidence custody under finite predicates. Historical case counts, lexical planning checks, signatures and stored PASS fields do not establish general mathematical or model-performance claims. Current historical pin mismatches remain unresolved custody, with no verified original transformation ledger.

## Claims

### ORR-C01 — The useful reusable mechanisms are explicit source/body encoding, typed boundary checks, duplicate-key refusal and scoped evidence publication;622 files are not622 new mechanisms.

Source-level finite contracts and historical cases only; no behavioral equivalence or unrestricted efficacy theorem.

### ORR-C02 — The amended typed-contract auditor explicitly preserves Unadmitted/HOLD and separates signed instruction scope from detector qualification.

Trusted compiler, fixture bank, callback and signer premises remain external. Receipt construction and15 finite controls do not establish global detector soundness.

### ORR-C03 — Several historical closeouts correctly preserve failed attempts, overlap, drift and limited authority instead of erasing negative evidence.

This endorses qualification practice, not complete historical custody or all claimed executions.

### ORR-C04 — Fresh confined controls validate all six fixed UTF-8 digest oracles and the245+99−55=289 count; they do not rerun the historical application/native systems.

Six literal SHA checks, fixed arithmetic and framing equality are bounded observations.

## Findings

### ORR-F01 — Current receipt bytes do not close many historical source/artifact bindings, and no verified original archive or transform ledger is available.

Across274 fully parsed JSON artifacts:22,828 recognized pin occurrences,1,175 resolve within frozen scope,55 match current raw bytes and zero match only LF/CRLF. The remaining1,120 resolved occurrences mismatch. Separately,291 checksum-list entries yield177 scoped resolutions and16 raw matches. Counts are occurrences, not independent unique artifacts, and inventories with duplicate keys use preserved diagnostic records.

These are current-checkout comparison limits, not evidence that original historical tests failed. LF/CRLF attempts do not recover unknown canonicalization; unresolved/out-of-scope references were not read.

Next: Mark affected historical binding unresolved; retain originals and compare only against a verified original ledger if one later appears.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/SHA256SUMS` 1–15; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/defect_hunter/SHA256SUMS` 1–12; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/syntax_prefilter/SHA256SUMS` 1–12; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/defect_hunter/native_adapter_hold_finding.json` 1–11

### ORR-F02 — Five current JSON files contain conflicting duplicate path keys, so ordinary object parsing silently drops declarations.

OEWAS007 root repeats adversary/entrypoints/cli.py with different hashes. Three equal native inventories repeat core_adversary Cargo.toml/exact.rs/lib.rs, collapsing3667 pairs to3664 keys; OEWAS009 repeats the same three, collapsing3677 to3674. Different sizes and hashes are preserved in diagnostics.

The duplicates are visible in current retained copies. Whether historical copies contained those same path collisions is unresolved; no origin/fraud claim.

Next: Refuse duplicate keys in future replay and retain the raw pair population. Never repair historical JSON in place.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas007_20261010/receipt.json` 158–158, 256–256; `operads/receipts/oewas007_20261010/scope_guard/native_host_build_final_source_inventory.json` 7021–7024, 7049–7052, 7061–7064, 14153–14156, 14225–14228, 14257–14260; `operads/receipts/oewas007_20261010/scope_guard/native_host_build_historical_lock_source_inventory.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON); `operads/receipts/oewas007_20261010/scope_guard/native_host_build_source_inventory.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON); `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/scope_guard/native_host_build_source_inventory.json` 7053–7056, 7081–7084, 7093–7096, 14193–14196, 14265–14268, 14297–14300

### ORR-F03 — The captured immune adapter treats an empty or wholly skipped YAML-spec directory as certified acceptance.

An existing empty directory yields yaml_files=[]; violations and screened stay empty; is_valid=True and self_tolerance_certified=True flow to ACCEPTED. Non-dict or missing-id/pattern specs are skipped. The scan does not enforce catalog admission, despite the admitted-antibody wording.

Historical baseline owner only; no claim that a current deployed owner still has this behavior. The finite empty-set path follows directly from code; no application execution.

Next: Require explicit admitted/nonempty applicable detector population and distinguish no evidence from CLEAR before reuse.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/defect_hunter/baseline_bytes/adversary/infrastructure/estates/oeeis_immune.py` 137–223, 299–309

### ORR-F04 — The retained defective native adapter rejects legitimate HOLD decisions that carry hit-spec evidence.

The condition (CLEAR and hits) or (specs and HOLD) rejects the documented HOLD+bounded-overclaim-hit case. Source-level logic is observable even though exact historical byte custody is separately unresolved.

A named preserved defect, not a fresh native reproduction or assertion about the repaired production owner. Its original declared source pin does not match current capture.

Next: Preserve this counterexample and its repair history; check HOLD/positive-hit and CLEAR/zero-hit as distinct wire cases.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/defect_hunter/native_adapter_hold_defect.snapshot` 168–178; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/defect_hunter/native_adapter_hold_finding.json` 1–11

### ORR-F05 — The initial candidate pharmacy snapshots do not jointly commit payload and catalog or verify returned seed writes.

Payload uses direct write_text; catalog uses a separate temporary replace. Seeder IDs take eight SHA hex digits, so general uniqueness is not proven; unrestricted colon framing and unchecked direct model construction retain separate admission assumptions.

Captured initial owners only. No concurrent fault injection or production repair performed. Candidate/noncandidate preservation in the seeder is useful but not complete transactional custody.

Next: Use verified candidate readback and an explicitly designed joint commit/recovery protocol before promoting this path into reusable publication.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/syntax_prefilter/pharmacy_catalog_initial.snapshot` 49–108; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/syntax_prefilter/pharmacy_models_initial.snapshot` 1–125; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/syntax_prefilter/pharmacy_seeder_initial.snapshot` 70–151

### ORR-F06 — Historical capture helpers can publish incomplete or post-only custody and the manifest writer writes before validating.

Manifest --write publishes rebuilt bytes before audit. run_python_check silently omits missing pin paths, stores before/after without enforcing equality, overwrites the same label and pins only some dependency versions. In-place mutation finally restoration is not crash recovery. The later body-regression launcher improves selected pinning and isolation but does not certify the entire environment.

These source observations concern retained launchers; none was run. A recorded before/after pair can be useful without constituting an atomic no-drift guarantee.

Next: Require all declared paths to exist, enforce pre/post consistency before success, use unique immutable outputs, and separate rollback/atomicity from ordinary finally restoration.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas007_20261010/planning_default_ic/observed_red_inputs/product_planning_office/use_cases/epic_manifest_audit.py.snapshot` 382–401; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/run_python_check.py` 27–37, 73–104; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/run_root_mutations.py` 1–62; `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/main_integration/body_regressions/run_body_regressions.py` 1–149

### ORR-F07 — Planning/TDD and prior-art PASS predicates certify lexical/custody structure, not executed tests, obstruction proofs or literature comprehension.

Marker presence, hashes, path sets and registry counts are reusable planning checks. The880-row catalog has uniform relevance_score=5 and unique locations; its11,138 reviewed-files claim is not independently substantiated by this audit.

The source itself often preserves SPEC_ONLY/RESEARCH_HOLD and false certification flags; the overclaim occurs only if downstream readers promote those statuses. Catalog descriptions/references were bounded, not fully read literature.

Next: Keep structural-audit status distinct from mathematical/empirical evidence and require actual owner/checker observations for promotion.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas007_20261010/planning_default_ic/observed_red_inputs/product_planning_office/use_cases/epic_tdd_plan_audit.py.snapshot` 1–71; `operads/receipts/oewas007_20261010/planning_default_ic/observed_red_inputs/product_planning_office/use_cases/prior_art_epic_audit.py.snapshot` 1–165; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/defect_hunter/baseline_bytes/product_planning_office/plans/epics/epic_relevance_5_prior_art.yaml` 1–55

### ORR-F08 — The before/after body-binding snapshots show a real encoding repair, while the later equal-concatenation test refinement is not a new historical RED.

Before payload binds only total instruction_bytes and supplied source metadata; equal-length body changes can leave it unchanged. After payload includes ordered_operand_bodies. The refined ab|c versus a|bc case has identical concatenation abc but different framed sequences; the older c|ab versus bc|a fixture lacks that same-concatenation premise.

Static exact-source difference plus pure literal framing check; no fresh compiler execution. Hash binding prevents the illustrated representation transplant under stated source metadata, not arbitrary semantic drift.

Next: Retain exact ordered-body JSON framing and the stronger boundary fixture; label post-repair oracle refinement honestly.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/body_binding/before_source.py` 255–275; `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/body_binding/after_source.py` 257–278; `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/body_binding/boundary_refinement_test_snapshot.py` 1–172; `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/body_binding/test_snapshot.py` 1–167; `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/body_binding/boundary_refinement_amendment.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON)

### ORR-F09 — The planar associativity witness checks ordered source/target boundaries, not equality of complete named operations or task behavior.

Nested substitution uses correct i+j offset and compares source_colours/target_colour. substitute constructs parenthesized names, so theta o_i (phi o_j psi) and (theta o_i phi) o_(i+j) psi need not have equal full dataclass names. Returned left composite therefore witnesses only checked fields.

Tuple-splice associativity is a useful typed-signature property. No universal executable-operad proof, behavior-preservation result or invalidity of the bounded kernel is inferred.

Next: Name the witness as boundary equality, or explicitly quotient names and supply denotational composition/unit laws before stronger promotion.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/planar_slot_laws/source_snapshot.py` 118–137, 153–189; `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/planar_slot_laws/test_snapshot.py` 1–216

### ORR-F10 — Historical mutation and independent-review counts need oracle and population qualification.

Readback mutation originally expected5 failures and was marked not killed; successor recognizes the same8-failure log and adds separately recorded guards. Three mutant runs reuse20 cases. Preflight403 changed zero files.245+99 overlaps by55. Independent negative inputs do not become production mutations, and earlier13-test mutation snapshots differ from later15-test audit controls.

No historical test was rerun. A killed finite mutant is local sensitivity evidence, not independence of every test or soundness of the checker.

Next: Report unique cases, exact mutation source/test bytes, infrastructure failures and oracle corrections separately; preserve failed predecessors.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/syntax_prefilter/mutations.json` 1–18; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/syntax_prefilter/mutations_verified.json` 1–46; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/root_mutation_preflight.json` 1–6; `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/planning_integration/README.md` 5–7; `operads/receipts/typed_contracts_20261010/independent/review.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON); `operads/receipts/typed_contract_audit_20261010/receipt.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON)

### ORR-F11 — GREEN/RED filenames and process exit codes alone do not identify successful or discriminating tests.

wire_green.log.gz decompresses to20 passed/2 errors caused by32767-character Windows environment limit. Native-composition green and planning green captures retain exit1. red_recursion exits0 and its own review calls it GREEN.

The gzip errors are pytest environment setup/teardown limits, not an observed parser acceptance bug. Bounded command metadata is not full stdout proof.

Next: Select evidence by actual assertion/failure and source binding, not filename or exit status; keep infrastructure errors outside behavioral RED counts.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas_002_20261010/wire_green.log.gz` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON); `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/receipt_selection_attempts.json` 1–7; `operads/receipts/oewas007_20261010/planning_default_ic/green.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON); `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/native_composition_green.command.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON); `operads/receipts/typed_contracts_20261010/cli/compiler_cli_red_recursion.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON); `operads/receipts/typed_contracts_20261010/cli/compiler_cli_review.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON)

### ORR-F12 — Several histories explicitly lack the exact intermediate source/test bytes required for retrospective reproduction.

Initial ingress RED source reconstruction is distinguished from exact pre-RED capture. Source preflight records three drifts; later test-drift receipt explicitly omits the current hash. Frozen50-case replay is separate from earlier45-case evidence and added controls.

A missing intermediate capture does not invalidate later separately pinned results, nor does it authorize replacing historical bytes with present code.

Next: Keep each attempt tied to its own custody level and preserve unknown intermediate hashes as unknown.

Evidence: `operads/receipts/oewas_001_ingress_20261010.json` (bounded parsed artifact; pointer in JSON); `operads/receipts/typed_contracts_20261010/independent/seal_preflight_source_drift.json` 1–21; `operads/receipts/typed_contracts_20261010/independent/seal_preflight_test_drift.json` 1–7; `operads/receipts/oewas009_20261011/receipt_selection_attempts.json` 1–7

## Fresh confined checks

All six fixed literal digests match.245+99−55=289. The equal-concatenation sequences ab|c and a|bc differ under JSON framing. All274 parsed JSON artifacts decode without nonfinite tokens; five contain conflicting duplicate path keys.22,828 pin occurrences include1,175 scoped resolutions/55 raw matches/zero normalized-only matches. Three checksum lists have291 valid distinct-within-list rows,177 scoped resolutions and16 raw matches. The880-row catalog has unique locations and all scores5; linked literature is not reviewed here. The compressed wire log retains20 passes and two pytest environment-size errors.

## Coverage and limits

- Every622 assigned path has an explicit disposition.351 full-source receipts include complete pinned baseline plus all differences and exact-equality transfers with context;271 bounded artifacts do not receive full-text credit.
- All executable/spec/narrative source bodies were covered. Large generated JSON, checksum lists,880-row catalog and compressed log retain explicit parsed/selected-value ceilings.
- No retained campaign, shell, pytest, native build/binary or provider program was executed. Fresh controls are isolated parsing, hashing and arithmetic. No private benchmark/seed/key access.
- Current raw/LF/CRLF mismatches are not proof of historical failure; no verified original operads/receipts archive/transform ledger is known. Other migration archives are not substitutes.
- Duplicate keys preserved by diagnostic hook; ordinary parsed populations use last values with explicit caveat. Recognized pin occurrences are not unique artifacts and unresolved/out-of-scope paths were not accessed.
- No efficacy, novelty, general task-preservation, detector adequacy, hostile-concurrency, mathematical kernel proof or independent-judge conclusion is licensed by stored self-reports/coverage/status labels.
- No original source, historical receipt or shared DAG/coverage/progress file was modified.

The JSON retains per-file raw SHA256, exact source ranges or bounded dispositions, contextual assessments, relationships and all claim evidence. The inspection/control helpers are read-only with respect to original sources.
